//============================================================
//  KARGAS IV - Mission trigger notes
//  Required Locations: P1~P4 Start, P5 Base, P5 Home, P5 Cerebrate, P5 Area, P5 Scout, P5 Intruder,
//                      P5 Spawn1~4, P1~P4 Hunt, Player Destination, AttackPoint1,
//                      P6 Base, P6 Home, P6 MiniBase, P6 Area, P6 Area Ground, P6 Area Air,
//                      P6 Scout, P6 Intruder, P6 Overmind, P6 Spawn1~4,
//                      P7 Base, P7 Home, P7 Area, P7 Area Ground, P7 Area Air,
//                      P7 Scout, P7 Intruder, P7 Overmind, P7 Spawn1~4,
//                      East Area, Mission2 Gate1~5, NewRoute1~15, Raid1~3, Waypoint1~5,
//                      P6 Alert, P7 Alert, P6 Alert Ping, P7 Alert Ping
//
//  P6/P7 Overmind locations require preplaced invincible Overmind Cocoons
//  owned by Player 6 / Player 7. The event disables invincibility on the selected one.
//
//  Timer rule: 12 DC tick = 1 second
//    10s = 120, 60s = 720, 90s = 1080, 3min = 2160, 4min = 2880, 5min = 3600
//
//  Player 8 DC roles
//    Terran Civilian      : zerg resource supply timer
//    Terran Marine        : P5 base defense cooldown
//    Terran Armory        : P6 base defense cooldown
//    Protoss Zealot       : P7 base defense cooldown
//    Terran Valkyrie      : mission phase flag (1=M1, 3=M2, 4=clear)
//    Terran Vulture       : mission2 elapsed timer
//    Protoss Probe        : P5 scout rotation index
//    Protoss Scout        : P6 scout rotation index
//    Protoss Carrier      : P7 scout rotation index
//    Terran Barracks      : P5 Hunt all-out attack timer
//    Terran Firebat       : P6 Hunt all-out attack timer
//    Protoss Arbiter      : P7 Hunt all-out attack timer
//    Terran Goliath       : P5 scout overlord cycle timer
//    Protoss Archon       : P6 scout overlord cycle timer
//    Protoss Dark Templar : P7 scout overlord cycle timer
//    Protoss Shuttle      : P1~P4 Hunt location refresh timer
//    Terran Ghost         : P6 MiniBase reveal timer
//    Terran Wraith        : mission1 late-wave cooldown
//    Terran Battlecruiser : P5 overlord supply cycle timer
//    Protoss Dragoon      : P6 overlord supply cycle timer
//    Protoss High Templar : P7 overlord supply cycle timer
//    Terran Science Vessel: player alliance refresh timer
//    Terran Dropship      : mission1 late-wave variant index
//    Protoss Corsair      : mission1 outer colony warning cooldown timer
//    Zerg Creep Colony    : mission1 P6/P7 creep colony warning counter
//    Zerg Zergling        : P6 mission2 penalty assault cooldown
//    Zerg Hydralisk       : P7 mission2 penalty assault cooldown
//    Zerg Lurker          : P6 mission2 penalty assault ready flag (0=idle, 1=spawning, 2=relay, 3=warning, 4=weakened warning, 5=weakened spawning, 6=pre-analysis queued, 7=weakened pre-analysis queued)
//    Zerg Mutalisk        : P7 mission2 penalty assault ready flag (0=idle, 1=spawning, 2=relay, 3=warning, 4=weakened warning, 5=weakened spawning, 6=pre-analysis queued, 7=weakened pre-analysis queued)
//    Zerg Ultralisk       : P6 mission2 penalty assault spawn step timer
//    Zerg Devourer        : P7 mission2 penalty assault spawn step timer
//    Zerg Scourge         : P6 mission2 penalty assault waypoint window timer
//    Zerg Guardian        : P7 mission2 penalty assault waypoint window timer
//    Protoss Reaver       : P6 mission2 penalty assault spawn stage index
//    Protoss Observer     : P7 mission2 penalty assault spawn stage index
//
//  Player 5 DC roles
//    Zerg Hatchery        : mission1 late-wave cooldown seed
//    Zerg Lair            : mission1 late-wave cooldown seed
//    Zerg Hive            : mission1 late-wave cooldown seed
//
//  Switch roles
//    Switch1 : defeat guard
//    Switch2 : resource raid sites revealed/spawned
//    Switch3 : Raid1 cleared
//    Switch4 : Raid2 cleared
//    Switch5 : Raid3 cleared
//    Switch6 : mission2 P7 airstrike one-time flag
//    Switch7 : mission1 multi scan revealers created
//    Switch8 : mission1 multi scan revealers removed
//    Switch9/10 : P6 mission2 penalty assault random selector
//    Switch11/12: P7 mission2 penalty assault random selector
//    Switch13   : mission1 P6/P7 creep colony warning display flag
//    Switch14~16: P6 mission2 penalty assault random waypoint selector
//    Switch17~19: P7 mission2 penalty assault random waypoint selector
//    Switch20/21: P6 mission2 penalty assault random Hunt selector
//    Switch22/23: P7 mission2 penalty assault random Hunt selector
//    Switch24   : P6 mission1 outer colony resource unlocked
//    Switch25   : P7 mission1 outer colony resource unlocked
//    Switch26   : mission2 first penalty assault order shuffle
//    Switch27/28: P6 mission2 penalty assault cooldown jitter
//    Switch29/30: P7 mission2 penalty assault cooldown jitter
//    Switch31   : P6 MainBase tech collapse main-assault lock
//    Switch32   : P7 MainBase tech collapse main-assault lock
//    Switch33   : P6 mission1 main assault cooldown enabled
//    Switch34   : P7 mission1 main assault cooldown enabled
//    Switch35   : P6 mission1 first tech-core assault boost used
//    Switch36   : P7 mission1 first tech-core assault boost used
//    Switch37   : P6 one-colony clear reward/message paid
//    Switch38   : P7 one-colony clear reward/message paid
//    Switch39   : mission2 overmind cocoon side selector (clear=P6, set=P7)
//    Switch40   : mission2 overmind cocoon active / reward unpaid
//    Switch41   : mission2 overmind cocoon weakened-assault pending
//    Switch42/43: P5 hunt all-out random target selector
//    Switch44/45: P6 hunt all-out random target selector
//    Switch46/47: P7 hunt all-out random target selector
//    Switch48   : P6 MiniBase revealed
//    Switch49   : P6 MiniBase reward/message paid
//    Switch50   : mission2 assault setup initialized
//    Switch51   : P6 mission2 penalty assault relay pulse open
//    Switch52   : P7 mission2 penalty assault relay pulse open
//    Switch53   : P6 mission2 penalty assault spawn rally pulse open
//    Switch54   : P7 mission2 penalty assault spawn rally pulse open
//    Switch55   : P6 AI activation requested
//    Switch56   : P7 AI activation requested
//    Switch57   : AttackPoint1 Cerebrate reward armed
//
//  Map editor settings
//    Player 5/6/7 Overlord upgrades must be configured in SCMDraft:
//    Pneumatized Carapace and Antennae = Level 1 + Researched.
//
//  Note: avoid Order("...", "Men", ...) for broad combat orders.
//============================================================
// 게임 시작 브리핑
Trigger("All Players"){
Conditions:
	Elapsed Time(At most, 1);
Actions:
	Play WAV("sound\\Misc\\TRescue.wav", 2000);
	Display Text Message(Always Display, " ");
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<13><03>==========================================");
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<13><03>작전 개시: 카르가스 IV");
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<13><03>==========================================");
	Set Mission Objectives("<03>미션 1: 첫 전선\n\n<04>- 목표: 방어선을 세우고 자원을 확보한 뒤 북서쪽 <11>주황색 저그<04> 군락 완전 파괴\n- 성공 조건: 북서쪽 <11>주황색 저그<04> 군락의 모든 해처리 / 레어 / 하이브 파괴\n- 지침: 기지를 안정화하고 업그레이드 후 군락을 압박할 것");
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Set To, 1);
}

Trigger("All Players"){
Conditions:
	Elapsed Time(At least, 4);
	Elapsed Time(At most, 6);
Actions:
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<03>카르가스 IV 작전사령관 호크: <04>현장 지휘관들, 교신 확인.");
}

Trigger("All Players"){
Conditions:
	Elapsed Time(At least, 9);
	Elapsed Time(At most, 11);
Actions:
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<04>지금 상태로는 오래 못 버틴다. 기지를 세우고 전선을 구축해야 한다.");
}

Trigger("All Players"){
Conditions:
	Elapsed Time(At least, 14);
	Elapsed Time(At most, 16);
Actions:
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<04>기지를 안정화하고 자원을 확보해라. 북서쪽 군락은 준비가 끝난 뒤 친다.");
}

Trigger("All Players"){
Conditions:
	Elapsed Time(At least, 21);
	Elapsed Time(At most, 23);
Actions:
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<1A>작전 정보장교 노바: <04>초기 정찰 보고입니다. 북서쪽 자원 지대에 대규모 군락이 확인됩니다.");
}

Trigger("All Players"){
Conditions:
	Elapsed Time(At least, 26);
	Elapsed Time(At most, 28);
Actions:
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<04>군락 주변 확장로가 막혀 있습니다. 그대로 두면 자원 확보가 늦어집니다.");
}

Trigger("All Players"){
Conditions:
	Elapsed Time(At least, 33);
	Elapsed Time(At most, 35);
Actions:
	Play WAV("sound\\Misc\\TRescue.wav", 1500);
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<1A>노바: <04>본진 외곽과 주 진입로도 확인됐습니다. 전술 지도에 표시합니다.");
}

Trigger("Player 1", "Player 2", "Player 3", "Player 4"){
Conditions:
	Elapsed Time(At least, 33);
	Elapsed Time(At most, 35);
Actions:
	Set Alliance Status("Player 9", Ally);
}

Trigger("Player 5", "Player 6", "Player 7", "Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Elapsed Time(At least, 33);
	Elapsed Time(At most, 35);
Actions:
	Set Alliance Status("Player 9", Enemy);
}

Trigger("Player 9"){
Conditions:
	Elapsed Time(At least, 33);
	Elapsed Time(At most, 35);
Actions:
	Set Alliance Status("Player 1", Ally);
	Set Alliance Status("Player 2", Ally);
	Set Alliance Status("Player 3", Ally);
	Set Alliance Status("Player 4", Ally);
	Set Alliance Status("Player 5", Enemy);
	Set Alliance Status("Player 6", Enemy);
	Set Alliance Status("Player 7", Enemy);
	Set Alliance Status("Player 8", Enemy);
}

Trigger("All Players"){

	Elapsed Time(At least, 38);
	Elapsed Time(At most, 40);

	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<03>호크: <04>표시된 입구가 첫 방어선이다. 저그가 전선을 넓히기 전에 여기서 막아라.");
}

//-----------------------------------------------------------------//
//  플레이어 팀킬 방지 - 동맹 상태 고정
//  P1~P4만 서로 Ally로 반복 갱신한다.
//  P9 입구 방어 건물 슬롯은 궤도스캔 시점에만 1회 설정한다.
//  Terran Science Vessel DC = 10초 주기 (120틱)
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

Trigger("Player 1"){
Conditions:
	Elapsed Time(At most, 1);
Actions:
	Set Alliance Status("Player 2", Ally);
	Set Alliance Status("Player 3", Ally);
	Set Alliance Status("Player 4", Ally);
}

Trigger("Player 2"){
Conditions:
	Elapsed Time(At most, 1);
Actions:
	Set Alliance Status("Player 1", Ally);
	Set Alliance Status("Player 3", Ally);
	Set Alliance Status("Player 4", Ally);
}

Trigger("Player 3"){
Conditions:
	Elapsed Time(At most, 1);
Actions:
	Set Alliance Status("Player 1", Ally);
	Set Alliance Status("Player 2", Ally);
	Set Alliance Status("Player 4", Ally);
}

Trigger("Player 4"){
Conditions:
	Elapsed Time(At most, 1);
Actions:
	Set Alliance Status("Player 1", Ally);
	Set Alliance Status("Player 2", Ally);
	Set Alliance Status("Player 3", Ally);
}

Trigger("Player 1"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Science Vessel", At least, 120);
Actions:
	Set Alliance Status("Player 2", Ally);
	Set Alliance Status("Player 3", Ally);
	Set Alliance Status("Player 4", Ally);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Trigger("Player 2"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Science Vessel", At least, 120);
Actions:
	Set Alliance Status("Player 1", Ally);
	Set Alliance Status("Player 3", Ally);
	Set Alliance Status("Player 4", Ally);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Science Vessel", At least, 120);
Actions:
	Set Alliance Status("Player 1", Ally);
	Set Alliance Status("Player 2", Ally);
	Set Alliance Status("Player 4", Ally);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Science Vessel", At least, 120);
Actions:
	Set Alliance Status("Player 1", Ally);
	Set Alliance Status("Player 2", Ally);
	Set Alliance Status("Player 3", Ally);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Science Vessel", At least, 120);
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Science Vessel", Set To, 0);
	Preserve Trigger();
}
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//
//  마스터 틱 - 모든 독립 타이머 동시 증가 (12회/초)
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Always();
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Civilian", Add, 1);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Marine", Add, 1);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Armory", Add, 1);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Barracks", Add, 1);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Zealot", Add, 1);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Firebat", Add, 1);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Arbiter", Add, 1);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Goliath", Add, 1);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Archon", Add, 1);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Dark Templar", Add, 1);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Shuttle", Add, 1);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Vulture", Add, 1);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Battlecruiser", Add, 1);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Dragoon", Add, 1);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss High Templar", Add, 1);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Science Vessel", Add, 1);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

// Mission 1 late-wave cooldown starts after 15 minutes.
Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Elapsed Time(At least, 900);
	Command("Player 5", "Zerg Cerebrate", At least, 1);
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Wraith", Add, 1);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

// Mission 2 main assault cooldowns advance during mission2.
Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 3);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Vulture", At least, 756);
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Zergling", Add, 1);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Hydralisk", Add, 1);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

// Mission 2 P6 MiniBase reveal timer starts after the first P6 main assault begins spawning.
Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 3);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Ghost", At least, 1);
	Switch("Switch48", Not Set);
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Ghost", Add, 1);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

// If an outer colony tech core is attacked during mission1, only that
// colony's main-assault cooldown starts advancing early.
Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Switch("Switch33", Set);
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Zergling", Add, 1);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Switch("Switch34", Set);
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Hydralisk", Add, 1);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

// Once an assault is queued, its spawn/relay timers may run in mission1 or mission2,
// but stop after the mission is complete.
Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", At least, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", At most, 3);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Lurker", At least, 1);
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Ultralisk", Add, 1);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Scourge", Add, 1);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", At least, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", At most, 3);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Mutalisk", At least, 1);
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Devourer", Add, 1);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Guardian", Add, 1);
	Preserve Trigger();
}
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//
//  저그 시작 보너스 + AI
//  P6/P7은 미션1 동안 자원을 0으로 묶고, 미션2부터 활성화한다.
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

Trigger("Player 5"){
Conditions:
	Elapsed Time(At most, 1);
Actions:
	Set Resources("Player 5", Set To, 999999, ore);
	Set Resources("Player 5", Set To, 999999, gas);
}

// 미션1 동안 P6/P7은 잠복 상태로 둔다.
Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Elapsed Time(At most, 2);
Actions:
	Set Switch("Switch24", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch25", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch55", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch56", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch57", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch13", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch35", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch36", clear);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Creep Colony", Set To, 0);
	Set Resources("Player 6", Set To, 0, ore);
	Set Resources("Player 6", Set To, 0, gas);
	Set Resources("Player 7", Set To, 0, ore);
	Set Resources("Player 7", Set To, 0, gas);
}

// AttackPoint1 optional Cerebrate reward target.
Trigger("Player 5"){
Conditions:
	Elapsed Time(At most, 1);
Actions:
	Create Unit("Player 5", "Zerg Cerebrate", 1, "AttackPoint1");
	Set Switch("Switch57", set);
}

// 각 저그 진영의 기본 AI 시작
Trigger("Player 5"){
Conditions:
	Elapsed Time(At least, 330);
Actions:
	Run AI Script At Location("ZSUx", "P5 Base");
}

// P6/P7은 Switch55/56이 켜질 때 자원을 먼저 받고 AI를 시작한다.

// Keep outer colonies resource-starved during mission1 until that colony is attacked.
Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Switch("Switch24", Not Set);
Actions:
	Set Resources("Player 6", Set To, 0, ore);
	Set Resources("Player 6", Set To, 0, gas);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Switch("Switch25", Not Set);
Actions:
	Set Resources("Player 7", Set To, 0, ore);
	Set Resources("Player 7", Set To, 0, gas);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

// 미션2 목표 표시 시 갈색/하얀색 군락을 깨운다.
Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 3);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Vulture", At least, 756);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Vulture", At most, 780);
Actions:
	Set Switch("Switch55", set);
	Set Switch("Switch56", set);
}

Trigger("Player 6"){
Conditions:
	Switch("Switch55", Set);
Actions:
	Set Resources("Player 6", Set To, 999999, ore);
	Set Resources("Player 6", Set To, 999999, gas);
	Run AI Script At Location("ZSUx", "P6 Base");
}

Trigger("Player 7"){
Conditions:
	Switch("Switch56", Set);
Actions:
	Set Resources("Player 7", Set To, 999999, ore);
	Set Resources("Player 7", Set To, 999999, gas);
	Run AI Script At Location("ZSUx", "P7 Base");
}
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//
//  자원 주기 보급 - Terran Civilian DC (60s = 720틱)
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

Trigger("Player 5"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Civilian", At least, 720);
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Civilian", Set To, 0);
	Set Resources("Player 5", Add, 30000, ore);
	Set Resources("Player 5", Add, 15000, gas);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Trigger("Player 5"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", At least, 3);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Civilian", At least, 720);
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Civilian", Set To, 0);

	Set Resources("Player 6", Add, 15000, gas);
	Set Resources("Player 7", Add, 30000, ore);
	Set Resources("Player 7", Add, 15000, gas);

}

//-----------------------------------------------------------------//
//  정찰 오버로드 사이클 - 각 군락별 독립 DC (120s = 1440틱)

//  P6: Protoss Archon / Protoss Scout
//  P7: Protoss Dark Templar / Protoss Carrier
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

//한 사이클 돌아가게 세팅
Trigger("Player 5"){
Conditions:
	Elapsed Time(At least, 175);
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Goliath", Set To, 2160);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Archon", Set To, 2160);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Dark Templar", Set To, 2160);
}

// Index 0 → P1 Hunt
Trigger("Player 5"){
Conditions:
	Elapsed Time(At least, 180);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Goliath", At least, 1440);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Probe", Exactly, 0);
Actions:
	Move Unit("Player 5", "Zerg Overlord", 1, "P5 Base", "P5 Scout");
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Overlord", "P5 Scout", "P1 Hunt", Move);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Goliath", Subtract, 1440);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Probe", Set To, 1);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

//  멀티 위치 궤도 스캔 안내
//  11분 럴커 공습 이후, 15분 대공습 이전에 P1~P4 멀티를 2분간 정찰.
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

// 멀티 위치 궤도 스캔 - 12분 30초
Trigger("All Players"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Elapsed Time(At least, 750);
Actions:
	Play WAV("sound\\Misc\\TRescue.wav", 1500);
	Minimap Ping("P1 Multi");
	Minimap Ping("P2 Multi");
	Minimap Ping("P3 Multi");
	Minimap Ping("P4 Multi");
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<1A>노바: <04>궤도 스캔 갱신. 각 전선 외곽에 아직 확보하지 않은 자원이 있습니다.");
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<03>호크: <04>본진 자원만으론 오래 못 버틴다. 여력이 생기면 추가 자원을 확보해라.");
}
Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Elapsed Time(At least, 750);
	Switch("Switch7", Not Set);
Actions:

	Create Unit("Player 1", "Map Revealer", 1, "P2 Multi");
	Create Unit("Player 1", "Map Revealer", 1, "P3 Multi");
	Create Unit("Player 1", "Map Revealer", 1, "P4 Multi");
	Create Unit("Player 2", "Map Revealer", 1, "P1 Multi");
	Create Unit("Player 2", "Map Revealer", 1, "P2 Multi");
	Create Unit("Player 2", "Map Revealer", 1, "P3 Multi");
	Create Unit("Player 2", "Map Revealer", 1, "P4 Multi");
	Create Unit("Player 3", "Map Revealer", 1, "P1 Multi");
	Create Unit("Player 3", "Map Revealer", 1, "P2 Multi");
	Create Unit("Player 3", "Map Revealer", 1, "P3 Multi");
	Create Unit("Player 3", "Map Revealer", 1, "P4 Multi");
	Create Unit("Player 4", "Map Revealer", 1, "P1 Multi");
	Create Unit("Player 4", "Map Revealer", 1, "P2 Multi");
	Create Unit("Player 4", "Map Revealer", 1, "P3 Multi");
	Create Unit("Player 4", "Map Revealer", 1, "P4 Multi");
	Set Switch("Switch7", set);
}

//  Dropship DC = 변형 인덱스 (0/1/2/3)
//
//  변형 A: 지상 스웜 (저글링/히드라/럴커 대량)

//  변형 C: 중장갑 돌파 (울트라/디파일러/히드라)

// 군락 테크 건물 붕괴 반격 보정 (1변종 = 2520틱, 시작 보정 = 10080틱)

//-----------------------------------------------------------------//
//  Mission 1 - dynamic building tracking and random all-out attack
//
//  Protoss Shuttle DC = Hunt location refresh timer (10s = 120)
//  Terran Barracks DC = P5 all-out attack timer (60s = 720)
//  Switch42/43 = P5 random Hunt target selector
//    clear/clear = P1, set/clear = P2, clear/set = P3, set/set = P4.
//  If a dead player is selected, the timer is not reset and rolls again.
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

Trigger("Player 5"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Command("Player 1", "Buildings", At least, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Shuttle", At least, 120);
Actions:
	Move Location("Player 1", "Buildings", "Anywhere", "P1 Hunt");
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Trigger("Player 5"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Command("Player 2", "Buildings", At least, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Shuttle", At least, 120);
Actions:
	Move Location("Player 2", "Buildings", "Anywhere", "P2 Hunt");
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Trigger("Player 5"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Command("Player 3", "Buildings", At least, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Shuttle", At least, 120);
Actions:
	Move Location("Player 3", "Buildings", "Anywhere", "P3 Hunt");
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Trigger("Player 5"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Command("Player 4", "Buildings", At least, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Shuttle", At least, 120);
Actions:
	Move Location("Player 4", "Buildings", "Anywhere", "P4 Hunt");
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Trigger("Player 5"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Shuttle", At least, 120);
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Shuttle", Set To, 0);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Trigger("Player 5"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Barracks", At least, 720);
Actions:
	Set Switch("Switch42", randomize);
	Set Switch("Switch43", randomize);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Trigger("Player 5"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Command("Player 1", "Buildings", At least, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Barracks", At least, 720);
	Switch("Switch42", Not Set);
	Switch("Switch43", Not Set);
Actions:
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Zergling", "Anywhere", "P1 Hunt", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Hydralisk", "Anywhere", "P1 Hunt", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Lurker", "Anywhere", "P1 Hunt", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Mutalisk", "Anywhere", "P1 Hunt", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Scourge", "Anywhere", "P1 Hunt", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Queen", "Anywhere", "P1 Hunt", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Defiler", "Anywhere", "P1 Hunt", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Ultralisk", "Anywhere", "P1 Hunt", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Guardian", "Anywhere", "P1 Hunt", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Devourer", "Anywhere", "P1 Hunt", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Infested Terran", "Anywhere", "P1 Hunt", Patrol);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Barracks", Set To, 0);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Trigger("Player 5"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Command("Player 2", "Buildings", At least, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Barracks", At least, 720);
	Switch("Switch42", Set);
	Switch("Switch43", Not Set);
Actions:
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Zergling", "Anywhere", "P2 Hunt", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Hydralisk", "Anywhere", "P2 Hunt", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Lurker", "Anywhere", "P2 Hunt", Patrol);

}

//-----------------------------------------------------------------//
//  Mission 1 - outer colony attack penalty
//
//  If players bypass the first colony and attack P6/P7 during mission1,
//  destroyed outer-colony structures feed resources back into that colony.
//  If either outer colony's Hatchery/Lair/Hive collapses, both P6 and P7 wake up.
//  The first tech-core collapse also primes one large assault from that colony only.
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

// Repeat the outer-colony intrusion warning at most once every 20 seconds.
Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Corsair", At least, 1);
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Corsair", Subtract, 1);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Trigger("Player 1"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Corsair", Exactly, 0);
	Bring("Player 1", "Men", "P6 Alert", At least, 1);
Actions:
	Play WAV("sound\\Misc\\TRescue.wav", 1500);
	Move Location("Player 1", "Men", "P6 Alert", "P6 Alert Ping");
	Minimap Ping("P6 Alert Ping");
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<03>호크: <04>선봉대, 이곳이 아니다. 북서쪽 <11>주황색 저그<04> 군락으로 병력을 돌려 공격해라.");
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<06>경고: 미션1 진행중 <11>주황색 저그<06>를 제외한 적을 공격할 경우 해당 적이 깨어납니다.");
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Corsair", Set To, 240);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

// P6 resource unlock warning.
Trigger("All Players"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Switch("Switch24", Not Set);
	Deaths("Player 6", "Zerg Sunken Colony", At least, 1);
Actions:
	Play WAV("sound\\Misc\\TRescue.wav", 2000);
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<1A>노바: <04>미확인 군락의 자원 흐름이 열렸습니다. 생체 반응이 급격히 증가합니다.");
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<03>호크: <04>방금 선을 넘었다. 저 군락은 이제 우리를 향해 전력을 모을 거다.");
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<06>경고: 이 군락이 깨어났습니다. 공격을 계속하면 미션2에 시작되는 공세가 미션1에 시작됩니다.");
	Set Switch("Switch24", set);
}

Trigger("All Players"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Switch("Switch24", Not Set);
	Deaths("Player 6", "Zerg Spore Colony", At least, 1);

// First Hatchery/Lair/Hive collapse primes one large assault from that colony only.
// Own these by P6/P7 so the boost runs before their death-counter consumers below.
Trigger("Player 6"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Switch("Switch35", Not Set);
	Deaths("Player 6", "Zerg Hatchery", At least, 1);
Actions:
	Set Switch("Switch35", set);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Zergling", Add, 11160);
}

//  미션1 클리어 -> 미션2 즉시 진입
//  Valkyrie DC: 1 -> 3, Vulture DC 0 리셋(미션2 경과 타이머)
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Bring("Player 5", "Zerg Hatchery", "P5 Base", At most, 0);
	Bring("Player 5", "Zerg Lair", "P5 Base", At most, 0);
	Bring("Player 5", "Zerg Hive", "P5 Base", At most, 0);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Set To, 3);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Vulture", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Shuttle", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Barracks", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Firebat", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Arbiter", Set To, 0);

//-----------------------------------------------------------------//
//  패배 - 단계와 관계없이 미션1/2 어느 단계든 본진 파괴 시 패배.

//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

Trigger("Player 1", "Player 2", "Player 3", "Player 4"){
Conditions:
	Command("Current Player", "Buildings", At most, 0);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", At least, 1);
	Elapsed Time(At least, 60);
	Switch("Switch1", Not Set);
Actions:
	Play WAV("sound\\Misc\\OutOfGas.wav", 2000);
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<13><03>==========================================");
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<13><03>작전 실패: 전선 붕괴");
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<13><03>==========================================");
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<03>호크: <04>무너진 건 방어선이지, 부대가 아니다. 교신 유지하고 잔존 병력은 후방으로 빠져라.");
	Wait(1500);
	Defeat();
}

//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

//-----------------------------------------------------------------//
//  Base defense response
//
//  P5: Terran Marine DC, P6: Terran Armory DC, P7: Protoss Zealot DC
//  Cooldown: 10s = 120 ticks.
//
//  P5 uses P5 Area for detection and P5 Intruder as the moving target.
//  P6/P7 Area Ground/Air locations are map-side filtered. Each response
//  moves the single Intruder location onto the detected unit, then orders
//  the matching defenders to patrol there.
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

Trigger("Player 5"){
Conditions:
	Bring("Player 1", "Men", "P5 Area", At least, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Marine", At least, 120);
Actions:
	Move Location("Player 1", "Men", "P5 Area", "P5 Intruder");
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Marine", Set To, 0);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Zergling", "Anywhere", "P5 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Hydralisk", "Anywhere", "P5 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Lurker", "Anywhere", "P5 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Mutalisk", "Anywhere", "P5 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Scourge", "Anywhere", "P5 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Ultralisk", "Anywhere", "P5 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Guardian", "Anywhere", "P5 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Devourer", "Anywhere", "P5 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Queen", "Anywhere", "P5 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Defiler", "Anywhere", "P5 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Infested Terran", "Anywhere", "P5 Intruder", Patrol);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Trigger("Player 5"){
Conditions:
	Bring("Player 2", "Men", "P5 Area", At least, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Marine", At least, 120);
Actions:
	Move Location("Player 2", "Men", "P5 Area", "P5 Intruder");
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Marine", Set To, 0);

// P6/P7 use separate Ground/Air area locations for detection. The single
// Intruder location is moved onto the detected player unit and then used
// as the patrol target. Triggers stay owned by the defending computer
// player, while P1~P4 are checked explicitly.

// P6: computer-owned defense triggers. Area Ground/Air locations filter target height.

Trigger("Player 6"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Armory", At least, 120);
	Bring("Player 1", "Men", "P6 Area Ground", At least, 1);
Actions:
	Move Location("Player 1", "Men", "P6 Area Ground", "P6 Intruder");
	Order("Player 6", "Zerg Zergling", "East Area", "P6 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 6", "Zerg Hydralisk", "East Area", "P6 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 6", "Zerg Lurker", "East Area", "P6 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 6", "Zerg Mutalisk", "East Area", "P6 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 6", "Zerg Ultralisk", "East Area", "P6 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 6", "Zerg Guardian", "East Area", "P6 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 6", "Zerg Queen", "East Area", "P6 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 6", "Zerg Defiler", "East Area", "P6 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 6", "Infested Terran", "East Area", "P6 Intruder", Patrol);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

//-----------------------------------------------------------------//
//  미션 2 진입 (Valkyrie = 3, Vulture = 0 리셋 직후)
//  목표: P6/P7 저그 군락 섬멸
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

Trigger("Player 1", "Player 2", "Player 3", "Player 4"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 3);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Vulture", At least, 756);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Vulture", At most, 780);
Actions:
	Play WAV("sound\\Misc\\TRescue.wav", 2500);
	Set Mission Objectives("<03>미션 2: 잔존 군락 섬멸\n\n<04>- 목표: 동북쪽 <15>갈색 저그<04> 군락과 동남쪽 <16>하얀색 저그<04> 군락 파괴\n- 성공 조건: 동북쪽 <15>갈색 저그<04> 군락 / 동남쪽 <16>하얀색 저그<04> 군락의 해처리 / 레어 / 하이브 파괴\n- 위치: 동북쪽 <15>갈색 저그<04> 군락 / 동남쪽 <16>하얀색 저그<04> 군락\n- 지침: 본진 방어선을 유지하면서 새 진입로를 통해 각 군락을 압박할 것");
	Create Unit("Current Player", "Map Revealer", 1, "P6 Home");
	Create Unit("Current Player", "Map Revealer", 1, "P7 Home");
	Minimap Ping("P6 Base");
	Minimap Ping("P7 Base");
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<13><03>==========================================");
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<13><03>미션 2: 잔존 군락 섬멸");
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<13><03>==========================================");
}

//-----------------------------------------------------------------//
//  Mission-independent - dynamic building tracking and random all-out attack
//
//  Protoss Shuttle DC = Hunt location refresh timer (10s = 120)
//  Terran Firebat DC = P6 all-out attack timer (60s = 720)
//  Protoss Arbiter DC = P7 all-out attack timer (60s = 720)
//  Switch44/45 = P6 random Hunt target selector
//  Switch46/47 = P7 random Hunt target selector
//    clear/clear = P1, set/clear = P2, clear/set = P3, set/set = P4.
//  If a dead player is selected, the timer is not reset and rolls again.
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

Trigger("Player 6"){
Conditions:
	Command("Player 1", "Buildings", At least, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Shuttle", At least, 120);
Actions:
	Move Location("Player 1", "Buildings", "Anywhere", "P1 Hunt");
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Trigger("Player 6"){
Conditions:
	Command("Player 2", "Buildings", At least, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Shuttle", At least, 120);
Actions:
	Move Location("Player 2", "Buildings", "Anywhere", "P2 Hunt");
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Trigger("Player 6"){

	Command("Player 3", "Buildings", At least, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Shuttle", At least, 120);

	Preserve Trigger();
}

	Preserve Trigger();
}

//-----------------------------------------------------------------//
//  Mission 2 - P6/P7 uncaptured-colony penalty assaults
//
//  12 DC tick = 1 second. Base cooldown is 960s = 11520 ticks.
//  Roll triggers subtract only the cooldown they consumed, so excess
//  progress from repeated Hatchery/Lair/Hive kills stays queued.
//  Hatchery/Lair/Hive kills add 80s worth of cooldown progress.
//
//  Waves may fire during mission1 or mission2, but not after victory.
//  They are rolled randomly, then spawned across Spawn1~4 in parallel
//  timed batches to reduce blocked creation space. Spawn timers only count up;
//  separate stage counters prevent repeated or chained creation. Air-heavy
//  waves are split into smaller sub-batches. Spawned groups
//  rally to Waypoint1~5. For 120s after the final batch,
//  P6/P7 assault units reaching the selected waypoint redirect
//  toward the already selected P1~P4 Hunt location.
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

// Reset assault counters once on mission2 entry.
Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 3);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Vulture", At most, 12);
	Switch("Switch50", Not Set);
Actions:
	Set Switch("Switch26", randomize);
	Set Switch("Switch27", set);
	Set Switch("Switch28", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch29", set);
	Set Switch("Switch30", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch37", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch38", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch39", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch40", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch41", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch48", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch49", clear);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Ghost", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Zergling", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Hydralisk", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Lurker", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Mutalisk", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Ultralisk", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Devourer", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Scourge", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Reaver", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Guardian", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Observer", Set To, 0);
}

// First penalty assaults are staggered after the mission2 assault cooldown opens
// at Vulture 756: one colony spawns at about 5:03 mission2 elapsed, the other at
// about 7:16. From the cooldown-open point, those are 4:00 and 6:13.
// Roll warnings fire 10 seconds before the first spawn batch.
Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 3);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Vulture", At most, 12);
	Switch("Switch50", Not Set);
	Switch("Switch26", Not Set);
	Switch("Switch31", Not Set);
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Zergling", Set To, 8760);
}

Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 3);

// If a colony's MainBase tech core collapses, lock that colony's main assault.
// This runs after the first-assault stagger setup so a collapsed colony stays at 0.
Trigger("Player 6"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 3);
	Switch("Switch31", Not Set);
	Bring("Player 6", "Zerg Hatchery", "P6 MainBase", At most, 0);
	Bring("Player 6", "Zerg Lair", "P6 MainBase", At most, 0);
	Bring("Player 6", "Zerg Hive", "P6 MainBase", At most, 0);
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Zergling", Set To, 0);
	Set Switch("Switch31", set);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

//-----------------------------------------------------------------//
//  Mission 2 - Overmind Cocoon pressure-relief event
//
//  Switch39 : selected cocoon side (Not Set=P6, Set=P7)
//  Switch40 : cocoon event active / reward unpaid
//  Switch41 : next main assault from selected side is weakened
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

// 12:00 selection. Overmind Cocoons are preplaced as invincible buildings.
// The event removes the unselected cocoon and disables invincibility on the selected one.
Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 3);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Vulture", At least, 8640);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Vulture", At most, 8664);
	Switch("Switch31", Not Set);
	Switch("Switch32", Not Set);
	Switch("Switch40", Not Set);
	Switch("Switch41", Not Set);
Actions:
	Set Switch("Switch39", randomize);
}

// Weakened roll: consume the selected side's next main assault cooldown
// before the normal random roll files can consume it.
// Pre-rolls show an early analysis cue; fallback rolls preserve the old timing if acceleration skips that window.

// P6 weakened pre-roll.

Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 3);
	Switch("Switch41", Set);
	Switch("Switch39", Not Set);
	Switch("Switch31", Not Set);

	Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Zergling", At most, 11159);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Lurker", Exactly, 0);
	Switch("Switch27", Not Set);
	Switch("Switch28", Not Set);

	Set Switch("Switch14", randomize);
	Set Switch("Switch15", randomize);
	Set Switch("Switch16", randomize);
	Set Switch("Switch20", randomize);
	Set Switch("Switch21", randomize);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Lurker", Set To, 7);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Ultralisk", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Scourge", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Reaver", Set To, 0);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

// P6 roll random wave - cooldown 15m30s - Hive active

	Preserve Trigger();
}

// End assault lock tail after 120 seconds.
Trigger("Player 7"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", At least, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", At most, 3);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Mutalisk", Exactly, 2);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Guardian", At least, 1441);
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Mutalisk", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Guardian", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Observer", Set To, 0);
	Set Switch("Switch52", clear);
	Preserve Trigger();
}
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//
//  Mission 2 - hatchery tech loss accelerates main assaults
//
//  Hatchery/Lair/Hive kills reduce that colony's own main assault
//  cooldown by 80s and grant players +100/+200/+300 minerals.
//  12 DC tick = 1 second, so 80s = 960.
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

Trigger("Player 1", "Player 2", "Player 3", "Player 4"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 3);
	Deaths("Player 6", "Zerg Hatchery", At least, 1);
Actions:
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<07>작전 보상: <15>갈색 저그<04> 해처리 파괴 보상. 미네랄 +100");
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Trigger("Player 6"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 3);
	Deaths("Player 6", "Zerg Hatchery", At least, 1);
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Zergling", Add, 960);
	Set Resources("Player 1", Add, 100, ore);
	Set Resources("Player 2", Add, 100, ore);
	Set Resources("Player 3", Add, 100, ore);
	Set Resources("Player 4", Add, 100, ore);
	Set Deaths("Player 6", "Zerg Hatchery", Subtract, 1);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Trigger("Player 1", "Player 2", "Player 3", "Player 4"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 3);
	Deaths("Player 6", "Zerg Lair", At least, 1);

//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

//  Switch49     : P6 MiniBase reward/message paid
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 3);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Ghost", At least, 2880);
	Switch("Switch48", Not Set);
Actions:
	Create Unit("Player 1", "Map Revealer", 1, "P6 MiniBase");
	Create Unit("Player 2", "Map Revealer", 1, "P6 MiniBase");
	Create Unit("Player 3", "Map Revealer", 1, "P6 MiniBase");
	Create Unit("Player 4", "Map Revealer", 1, "P6 MiniBase");
	Set Switch("Switch48", set);
}

Trigger("Player 1", "Player 2", "Player 3", "Player 4"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 3);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Ghost", At least, 2880);
	Switch("Switch48", Set);
Actions:
	Minimap Ping("P6 MiniBase");
	Set Mission Objectives("<03>미션 2: 잔존 군락 섬멸\n\n<04>- 목표: 동북쪽 <15>갈색 저그<04> 군락과 동남쪽 <16>하얀색 저그<04> 군락 파괴\n- 성공 조건: 동북쪽 <15>갈색 저그<04> 군락 / 동남쪽 <16>하얀색 저그<04> 군락의 해처리 / 레어 / 하이브 파괴\n- 보조 목표: 동북쪽 외곽집결지 파괴\n- 위치: 동북쪽 <15>갈색 저그<04> 군락 / 동남쪽 <16>하얀색 저그<04> 군락\n- 지침: 본진 방어선을 유지하면서 새 진입로를 통해 각 군락을 압박할 것");
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<1A>노바: <04>동북쪽 외곽에서 공세 집결 신호를 역추적했습니다. 좌표를 전술 지도에 표시합니다.");
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<03>호크: <04>표시 지점이 다음 타격 목표다. 가능한 병력부터 보내 집결지를 파괴해라.");

//-----------------------------------------------------------------//
//  [TEST MODE] Rotating player resource dashboard
//
//  Shows the original player mineral leaderboard during the first
//  half of the dashboard cycle. Pair with 94_test_resource_dashboard_02_dc.txt
//  to alternate between resources and the mission 2 assault DC dashboard.
//
//  Player 8 / Zerg Extractor = test dashboard cycle timer.
//  0~59 ticks: resources, 60~119 ticks: M2 assault DC, then reset.
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Always();
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Extractor", Add, 1);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Extractor", At least, 120);
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Extractor", Set To, 0);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Trigger("Player 1", "Player 2", "Player 3", "Player 4"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Extractor", At most, 59);
Actions:
	Leader Board Resources("TEST Minerals", ore);
	Leaderboard Computer Players(enabled);
	Preserve Trigger();
}
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//
//  [TEST MODE] Rotating mission 2 main assault DC dashboard
//
//  Standard StarCraft leaderboards cannot read death counters directly.
//  This test dashboard mirrors the two mission 2 main-assault cooldown
//  DCs into custom score, then restores the source DCs in the same pass.
//
//  Shown during the second half of the cycle driven by
//  94_test_resource_dashboard_01_resources.txt.
//
//  P6 custom score = Player 8 / Zerg Zergling DC.
//  P7 custom score = Player 8 / Zerg Hydralisk DC.
//  Temp DCs: Player 8 / Zerg Spawning Pool, Zerg Evolution Chamber.
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Always();
Actions:
	Set Score("Player 6", Set To, 0, Custom);
	Set Score("Player 7", Set To, 0, Custom);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Spawning Pool", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Evolution Chamber", Set To, 0);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

//-----------------------------------------------------------------//
//  [TEST MODE / CHEAT] Spawn 2 control groups of Jim Raynor at P1 Start
//  Included for manual test builds only.
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

Trigger("Player 1"){
Conditions:
	Elapsed Time(At most, 1);
Actions:
	Create Unit("Player 1", "Jim Raynor (Marine)", 24, "P1 Start");
}
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//
//  [TEST MODE / CHEAT] 게임 시작 시 P1에게 톰 카잔스키 36기 지급
//
//  켜는 법: 이 파일을 Triggers/ 폴더로 복사
//  끄는 법: Triggers/ 에서 제거
//
//  주의: 36기를 한 location에 몰아 스폰하면 stacking으로 일부 멀리
//        밀려날 수 있음. 테스트 목적이므로 P1 본진(P1 Start 등) 사용.
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

Trigger("Player 1"){
Conditions:
	Elapsed Time(At most, 1);
Actions:
	Create Unit("Player 1", "Tom Kazansky (Wraith)", 36, "Anywhere");
}
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//
//  [TEST MODE] 플레이어 절대 패배 방지 (스위치 방식)
//
//  켜는 법: 이 파일을 Triggers/ 폴더로 복사
//  끄는 법: Triggers/ 에서 제거
//
//  동작 원리:
//    게임 시작 직후 Switch1 을 set.
//    12_mission1_to_mission2_transition.txt 의 패배 트리거에 Switch("Switch1", cleared) 조건이
//    있으므로, 스위치가 set 상태인 한 Defeat()가 발동하지 않음.
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

Trigger("Player 5"){
Conditions:
	Elapsed Time(At most, 1);
Actions:
	Set Switch("Switch1", set);
}
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//
//  [TEST MODE] 컴퓨터(P5, P6) → 플레이어(P1~P4) 시야 공유
//  (플레이어가 컴퓨터의 시야를 가진다)
//
//  켜는 법: 이 파일을 Triggers/ 폴더로 복사
//  끄는 법: Triggers/ 에서 제거
//
//  사용 스크립트 (AI → 해당 플레이어로 시야 공유):
//    "Turn ON Shared Vision of current player with Player 1"
//    "Turn ON Shared Vision of current player with Player 2"
//    "Turn ON Shared Vision of current player with Player 3"
//    "Turn ON Shared Vision of current player with Player 4"
//
//  주의: 위 스크립트 이름이 SCMDraft 드롭다운에 정확히 그대로 있는지
//        확인할 것. 표기가 다르면 적절히 교체.
//        ("of [SHARER] with [RECEIVER]" 패턴으로 추정)
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

Trigger("Player 1", "Player 2", "Player 3", "Player 4"){
Conditions:
	Elapsed Time(At most, 1);
Actions:
	Run AI Script("+Vi4");
	Run AI Script("+Vi5");
	Run AI Script("+Vi6");
	Run AI Script("+Vi7");
}
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//
//  터보 트리거 (Hyper Trigger) × 4 - Player 8 소유
//
//  원리: Wait 액션 직전에 모든 트리거 조건을 재검사 → Wait(0) 다수로
//        초당 ~12회 트리거 평가 강제. 12 DC tick = 1초 공식 성립.
//
//  4개 배치 이유 (NEO 갭 곱연산):
//    NEO 사이클 = (Wait 개수 + 1) ^ (트리거 개수)
//    1개: 63 사이클 ≈ 5초마다 끊김 (사실상 무효)
//    4개: 63^4 ≈ 1575만 사이클 ≈ 15일 이상 무중단 → 어떤 게임 길이도 안전
//
//  Player 8 단독 소유: 다른 Wait를 사용하는 트리거가 P8에 없음을 확인.
//  P8은 컴퓨터 중립 슬롯이라 게임 도중 이탈 위험 없음.
//
//  주의: P8에는 Wait를 사용하는 다른 트리거를 추가하지 말 것
//        (Wait 블록 발생 시 hyper 효과 멈춤).
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Always();
Actions:
	Wait(0);
	Wait(0);
	Wait(0);
	Wait(0);
	Wait(0);
	Wait(0);
	Wait(0);
	Wait(0);
	Wait(0);
	Wait(0);
	Wait(0);
